# Graph Report - AbstrHActor  (2026-08-02)

## Corpus Check
- 2281 files · ~2,201,827 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 34523 nodes · 62893 edges · 2469 communities (2169 shown, 300 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 91% EXTRACTED · 9% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5709 edges (avg confidence: 0.51)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `368f4a01`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
